## [Unreleased]

### Changed

- **docs:** README refreshed with v1.10.0+ baseline + updated platform-state pitch ([#132](https://github.com/mgoodric/security-atlas/issues/132)) — refreshes the README's "v1 complete" callout to reflect operator-grade reality (120+ slices merged, v1.10.0 current release, audit-log trio 124+125+126+129+130, CI hardening trilogy 117+127+128). Adds a new "What's new in v1.10.0" section with two-bullet capability batch summaries. Drops the 1.8 MB `flow-create-control.gif` (slice 132 scope: static PNG only) and the "What it looks like in motion" subsection — the dashboard → control-detail flow is implicit from the side-by-side static screenshots. Hardens the slice-057 capture pipeline at `web/scripts/capture-readme-screenshots.ts` with the AC-2 safety gate: `assertCaptureSafe()` refuses to run unless `ATLAS_DEMO_SEED=1` is set AND the upstream HTTP target resolves to a loopback / RFC1918 private-range address. New test file `web/scripts/capture-readme-screenshots.test.ts` (26 cases — `isLoopbackOrPrivate` covers 14 branches; `assertCaptureSafe` covers 12) locks the per-branch behavior so a refactor cannot widen the admit set silently — the gate is the load-bearing information-disclosure mitigation per slice 132's threat model (every captured PNG is permanent public PII-leak vector). New `scripts/**/*.test.ts` include pattern added to `web/vitest.config.ts`. The `just refresh-screenshots` recipe drops the ffmpeg/GIF post-step, promotes pngquant from optional to required, exports `ATLAS_DEMO_SEED=1` into the script invocation, and adds a per-file + total budget gate (page ≤ 200K, hero ≤ 350K, total ≤ 2M — exit non-zero on violation). Per-image budget audit: total 288 KB / 2048 KB cap (14% utilization). All 22 relative README links + 10 image references validated. JUDGMENT-type slice — decisions log at `docs/audit-log/132-readme-refresh-decisions.md` records D1 the seed-fixture choice (slice-057 hermetic stub-server is the documented equivalent the slice doc AC-3 admits), D2 the per-image budget table, D3 the README sections changed, D4 the screenshots considered but cut (GIF + login-page + audit-log + risk-hierarchy), D5 the capture-vs-restore call (the capture pipeline ran but surfaced a non-slice-132 BFF cookie regression; restored slice-057 PNGs against the same component tree — captured 2026-05-14, no breaking dashboard / control-detail / audit-workspace / board-pack-preview UI change since), D6 the per-PNG provenance + manual-review audit (P0-A1/A2/A3 verified each), D7 the anti-criteria compliance table, D8 the test-coverage rationale (26 cases for a one-function gate because a loosened gate publishes data permanently). Anti-criteria honored (P0): no real customer data in any PNG (A1); no emails / IPs / tokens / real-org names (A2); demo-seed PII synthetic-only (A3); ≤ 2 MB total + no `git lfs` (A4); no docs/getting-started/ or docs/walkthroughs/ edits — slice 133 + 134 own those (A5); no CLAUDE.md edit — separate plain doc-PR per `/idea-to-slice` skill rule (A6); no logo asset regeneration (A7); neutral `test-*` fixture literals only (A8).

### Fixed

- **e2e:** unmask + fix 4 specs blocked by slice 119 polarity flip ([#123](https://github.com/mgoodric/security-atlas/issues/123)) — closes the diagnose-heavy spillover from slice 119's port-3000 fix. Once Playwright actually executed specs again post-119, four specs surfaced as failing: `auth-open-redirect.spec.ts`, `first-time-login.spec.ts`, `logo-render.spec.ts`, `security-headers.spec.ts`. Per-spec diagnoses + fixes — all in production code, NONE in spec body (anti-criteria P0-A1 / P0-A2 honored; no `.skip()` / `.fixme()` / assertion relaxation):
  - **`security-headers.spec.ts`** — the slice-087 `internal/api/securityheaders/middleware.go` only covered atlas (Go) responses on port :8080, but Playwright drives the browser against Next.js on :3000. Browser-served HTML carried NONE of the five hardening headers. Fix: add an `applySecurityHeaders(res)` block to `web/proxy.ts` mirroring the slice-087 directives byte-for-byte (HSTS 1y + includeSubDomains, X-Content-Type-Options nosniff, X-Frame-Options DENY, Referrer-Policy strict-origin-when-cross-origin, CSP report-only — same hydration-violation D1 reason for report-only). Applied to every Next.js response: `/login`, `/dashboard`, BFF routes, AND public static assets + redirects (defense-in-depth so a 307-followed browser doesn't see one un-headered hop). 4 new vitest cases in `web/proxy.test.ts` lock the contract.
  - **`logo-render.spec.ts`** — the proxy matcher excluded `_next/static`, `_next/image`, and `favicon.ico` but NOT the other public assets the login page + Metadata API reference: `og-image.png`, `twitter-card.png`, `icon-192.png`, `icon-512.png`, `apple-touch-icon.png`, `logo-light.svg`, `logo-dark.svg`. Unauthenticated `page.request.get("/og-image.png")` was 307→200-text/html (the login page render), breaking `expect(...).toBe("image/png")`. Real OG scrapers would have seen the same broken unfurl. Fix: explicit `PUBLIC_STATIC_FILES` Set allow-list in `web/proxy.ts` — short + literal per the P0-A1 exact-equality discipline (a regex `\.(png|svg|ico)$` would expose any future tenant-scoped asset). 8 new vitest cases including an adjacent-path negative (`/og-image-suffix.png` still redirects).
  - **`first-time-login.spec.ts`** — the slice-073 implementation fetched `/v1/install-state` from the login page's Server Component using `apiBaseURL()`. The spec mocked via `page.route("**/v1/install-state")` — but Playwright's `page.route()` only intercepts BROWSER network traffic, NEVER server-side `fetch()` from an RSC. The mock never fired; the spec hit the real atlas response (`first_install: false` post-seed-harness), hiding the card, timing out `toBeVisible`. Fix: new BFF route at `web/app/api/install-state/route.ts` (mirrors the slice-072 `/api/version` pattern — public upstream, no bearer forwarded, 5xx → safe default per P0-A5) + extracted client island `web/components/first-install-card.tsx` that fetches the BFF from the browser. Spec mock pattern updated from `**/v1/install-state` → `**/api/install-state` (the only spec-side change — the assertion is unchanged; the mock URL was wrong about the architecture). 7 new vitest cases on the BFF route.
  - **`auth-open-redirect.spec.ts`** — self-resolved by slice 122's `api_keys_token_hash_unique` parallel-worker race fix. The auth-spec failure trace had been a downstream symptom of the seed-harness `ON CONFLICT (token_hash) DO NOTHING` gap: one of two parallel Playwright workers' `seedFromFixture()` calls would lose the race and exit non-zero, deauthenticating the spec's bearer-cookie injection before sign-in completed. With slice 122 merged, the seed is idempotent across workers and the `signIn → safeRedirectTarget → /dashboard` flow completes correctly. No production-code change needed for this spec; the slice-086 `safeRedirectTarget` defense was already correct.
  - **Per-spec last-passing audit (AC-4):** all four specs were authored under the post-slice-079 quarantine (`continue-on-error: true`) where Playwright failures were silenced; slice 119 flipped the polarity but the underlying bugs were never green in CI for these specs — they were _masked_, not _regressed_. `git log` per spec shows: `security-headers.spec.ts` (slice 087, never executed against real Next.js port :3000 until 119); `logo-render.spec.ts` (slice 075, public-asset path never exercised until 119); `first-time-login.spec.ts` (slice 073, mock-URL bug present from authoring but masked by `continue-on-error`); `auth-open-redirect.spec.ts` (slice 086, masked by the slice-082 `api_keys` race fixed in slice 122). All four were "born red" rather than regressed from a green baseline.
  - Decisions log at `docs/audit-log/123-investigate-4-unmasked-e2e-spec-failures-decisions.md` records the per-spec root-cause analysis + the production-code-vs-spec-fix scoping call + the shared-vs-independent-cause finding. No spillover slices filed: each of the four root causes was small-surface and orthogonal.

### Added

- **infra:** SHA-pin every GitHub Action across all workflows + BLOCKING `actions-pin-check` CI guard ([#128](https://github.com/mgoodric/security-atlas/issues/128)) — closes the supply-chain gap that any non-SHA-pinned `uses:` line in any workflow exposes the project to the tag-jacking attack class. Extends slice 117's SHA-pin convention (which previously covered only `step-security/harden-runner`) to every action in every workflow: 26 unique upstream repos resolved through `gh api repos/<repo>/git/refs/tags/<tag>` (8 of them annotated-tag types that needed one extra dereference hop to the commit SHA — `actions/attest-build-provenance`, `github/codeql-action` covering 3 sub-paths, `bufbuild/buf-action`, `astral-sh/setup-uv`, `azure/setup-helm`, `aquasecurity/trivy-action`, `googleapis/release-please-action`, `sigstore/cosign-installer`). Every swept line preserves the `# <tag>` trailing comment for human readability + Dependabot's update convention. NO action version bumps (P0-A1 honored — Dependabot's job). NO workflow semantic changes — `if:` / `with:` / `env:` blocks unchanged byte-for-byte. New `scripts/check-action-pins.sh` walks all workflow files, extracts every `uses:` line (both named-step `uses: ...` and compact list-item `- uses: ...` YAML forms), asserts each ref is a 40-char lowercase-hex SHA, exits 1 with a per-line diagnostic on violation. New `scripts/check-action-pins_test.sh` covers 7 cases (pinned, tag-pinned, mixed, short-SHA, missing-dir, empty-dir, reproducibility) with 15 assertions; runs without network via the `ATLAS_WORKFLOWS_DIR` env override. New BLOCKING CI job `actions-pin-check` in `.github/workflows/ci.yml` runs the script on every PR + push to main — failure FAILS the build (unlike slices 069/089/109/120/127 which use the informational `continue-on-error: true` pattern; slice 128 is blocking because the supply-chain discipline must hold continuously). New `actions-pin-check` context added to `.github/branch-protection.json` `required_status_checks.contexts` with a `$additions_from_slice_128` annotation block recording the BLOCKING rationale; operator post-merge step is to run `bash scripts/apply-branch-protection.sh` (slice 127's apply ritual) to push the new contexts list to live GitHub branch-protection on `main`. New CONTRIBUTING.md "Action pinning" subsection documents the `uses: <action>@<sha> # <tag>` format + the `gh api` lookup procedure (with annotated-tag dereference) + the BLOCKING CI guard + the local repro. Decisions log at `docs/audit-log/128-sha-pin-all-github-actions-decisions.md` records D1 the per-action SHA resolution table (26 entries — the auditable sweep-time snapshot), D2 the Dependabot post-merge verification plan, D3 the slice-127 coordination outcome (file change + operator-apply-ritual decoupling), D4 the annotated-tag dereference edge cases. AFK-type slice; threat model HAS-MITIGATIONS — the slice IS the tag-jacking mitigation. Anti-criteria honored (P0): NO action version bumps (A1); full 40-char SHA only — no short SHAs (A2); `# <tag>` comment preserved on every line (A3); no semantic workflow changes (A5); SHAs resolved from upstream maintainer repos via `gh api` (A6); neutral test fixtures only — no vendor-prefixed tokens (A7).

- **infra:** Reconciled branch-protection drift; added drift-detect CI job + apply-ritual docs ([#127](https://github.com/mgoodric/security-atlas/issues/127)) — closes the file ↔ live drift between `.github/branch-protection.json` (the in-tree source-of-truth from slice 050 AC-11) and the live GitHub branch-protection config on `main` that caused four PRs to sit held for hours on a phantom-blocker rationale during the 2026-05-17/18 cascade-unblock session. JUDGMENT D1 picked option **(a)** edit-file-to-match-live: removes `Frontend · vitest` and `Frontend · Playwright e2e` from the file's `required_status_checks.contexts` list (which is what live actually enforces today; documented in a new `$deviations_from_slice_069` annotation block in the file). Option (b) — re-apply file to live to restore enforcement of those two checks — is deferred until slice 123 lands and unblocks the 4 still-broken e2e specs (otherwise (b) would block every PR including slice 127's own on known-failing specs; slice doc line 109). New `scripts/apply-branch-protection.sh` (idempotent — P0-A2 — strips `$`-prefixed annotation keys before PUT, re-reads live to verify convergence, returns exit 3 if GitHub silently drops a context the file lists). New `scripts/check-branch-protection-drift.sh` (local repro + the worker for the new CI job; exit 0 in sync / 1 drift / 2 env error; supports `ATLAS_FIXTURE_FILE` + `ATLAS_FIXTURE_LIVE` env overrides for offline testing). New `scripts/check-branch-protection-drift_test.sh` covers 6 cases (in-sync, drift, malformed JSON, missing-list, missing-live-fixture, reproducibility) with 13 assertions. New `branch-protection-drift` informational CI job in `.github/workflows/ci.yml` (`continue-on-error: true`, NOT in `required_status_checks.contexts` per P0-A1 — chicken-and-egg avoidance) runs on every PR + push to main, posts a sticky comment via the slice-120 hidden-marker pattern (`<!-- atlas-branch-protection-drift-marker -->`) on drift, uploads `drift.{stdout,stderr}` + `comment-body.md` as workflow artifacts. CONTRIBUTING.md "Branch protection" subsection replaces the stale ≥1-approving-review block from slice 050 with current 10-check ruleset + apply ritual + drift-detect signal + local repro instructions. JUDGMENT-type slice — decisions log at `docs/audit-log/127-branch-protection-drift-fix-decisions.md` records 6 design calls including the drift snapshot (D0), the reconcile direction (D1), the stub-twin omission rationale (D2), the AC-2 diff command correction (D3 — the literal command in the slice doc has a typo; this slice ships the corrected form), the apply-script vs inline-workflow trade-off (D4), and where the apply ritual is documented (D5). Anti-criteria honored (P0): drift-detect job is informational not required (A1); apply script is idempotent (A2); new job not in required-checks (A3); neutral test fixtures only — no vendor-prefixed tokens (A4); option (a) capitulation explicitly justified in the `$deviations_from_slice_069` annotation + decisions log D1 (A5); P0-A6 verified via `git log --all --follow .github/branch-protection.json` — the missing contexts originated as un-applied additions from slice 069, not as deliberate human removals from live (D0).

- **frontend:** Admin `/audit-log` page consuming the unified aggregation endpoint ([#125](https://github.com/mgoodric/security-atlas/issues/125)) — new top-level route at `/audit-log` exposes the slice-124 `GET /v1/admin/audit-log/unified` endpoint as a filterable, paginated, read-only table for admins (today) and for auditors / grc_engineers once spillover slice 130 lands. Server component shell at `web/app/audit-log/page.tsx` + TanStack-Query-driven client island at `web/app/audit-log/page-client.tsx`. Filters (from, to, actor free-text, kind multi-select chips) serialize to URL query params (`?from=...&to=...&actor=...&kind=evidence,me`) so back/forward + share-links restore the same view. Cursor-paginated infinite scroll via IntersectionObserver auto-loads up to 10 pages (10,000 rows); after the ceiling the operator must click "Load more" to make further cost visible (P0-A3 / threat-model DoS posture). Row expand-for-payload via a hand-rolled details-row pattern (no new shadcn primitive dependency — D5 in the decisions log). Date pickers use native `<input type="date">` (no calendar library — D4). Each row shows `occurred_at` (local time + UTC tooltip via `<time dateTime>`), `actor_id` truncated to first 8 chars with full UUID on hover (D1 — spillover slice 129 files the backend `actor_name` extension), `kind` badge, `target_type`, `target_id`, `action`, and a toggle-expand for the raw `payload_json`. Route guard: server-component layout at `web/app/audit-log/layout.tsx` issues a `/api/admin/me` preflight server-side and `redirect()`s non-admins to `/dashboard?error=admin-only` (D7 — the slice doc says `middleware.ts`; the project's Next.js 16 file is `proxy.ts`, which gates only the cookie-presence check; role membership is checked at the layout level per the slice 060 pattern). The route-level guard is the FIRST line of defense; the slice-124 backend 403 is defense-in-depth (P0-A2). New BFF route at `web/app/api/audit-log/unified/route.ts` forwards the request to the platform backend with `Authorization: Bearer <bearer>` only — NOT extending the slice-110 `atlas_session` cookie forward surface (D8 narrow-scope rule). Typed client at `web/lib/api/audit-log.ts` exports `AUDIT_LOG_KINDS` (the canonical 9-value enum), `MAX_WINDOW_DAYS = 90` (single-source-of-truth for the backend cap), `buildUnifiedQuery`, `fetchUnifiedAuditLog`, and `AuditLogFetchError`. New vitest at `web/app/api/audit-log/unified/route.test.ts` (6 tests: 401-no-cookie, happy-path bearer + query forward + no Cookie header, 400 window invalid pass-through, 400 missing param pass-through, 403 non-admin pass-through, atlas_session cookie present but NOT forwarded slice-110 P0-A2 guard); new vitest at `web/lib/api/audit-log.test.ts` (8 tests: required params verbatim, drops empty optional fields, CSV kind join, actor trim, cursor passthrough, canonical 9-kind enum, MAX_WINDOW_DAYS == 90, AuditLogFetchError carries status). New Playwright e2e spec at `web/e2e/audit-log.spec.ts` (5 tests: admin sees rows, non-admin redirected, filter updates URL, cursor/pagination wire, P0-A1 no-edit-affordance) following the slice-079 quarantine pattern pending the broader bring-up. New seed fixture `fixtures/e2e/audit-log.sql` seeds two `feature_flag_audit_log` rows + one `evidence_audit_log` row across the demo tenant + extends `web/e2e/seed.ts` `FixtureName` to include `audit-log`. The legacy `/admin/audit` scaffold (slice 060) coexists unchanged — different path, different audience, different information layer (D10). AFK-type slice. Decisions log at `docs/audit-log/125-audit-log-page-frontend-decisions.md` records the 10 JUDGMENT calls. Two spillovers filed: slice 129 (extend slice-124 endpoint with `actor_name`) + slice 130 (extend `/api/admin/me` with role enumeration so auditors can reach the page). Anti-criteria honored (P0): read-only UI (P0-A1 — no inline edit / delete affordances anywhere); route-level role guard runs server-side BEFORE the page renders (P0-A2); UI clamps the date range at 90 days to mirror the backend cap (P0-A3); neutral `test-*` fixture tokens only (P0-A4 GitGuardian guard).
- **observability:** external audit-log sink with backpressure-to-fallback ([#126](https://github.com/mgoodric/security-atlas/issues/126)) — closes the constitutional gap that an in-app admin can read + craft rows in the per-domain audit-log tables under slice 036's RLS policy. New `internal/audit/sink/` package exposes `Emit(ctx, unifiedlog.Entry)` (void return — D8: caller cannot mishandle a sink error) that fans out from each of the nine per-domain audit-log INSERT sites (`decision_audit_log`, `evidence_audit_log`, `exception_audit_log`, `sample_audit_log`, `audit_period_audit_log`, `aggregation_rule_audit_log`, `feature_flag_audit_log`, `me_audit_log`, `walkthrough_audit_log`) to a maintainer-configured JSONL file with per-record HMAC-SHA256 integrity tag. The canonical `unifiedlog.Entry` shape from slice 124 is re-used verbatim (D2): no struct duplication, the sink record format and the in-app aggregator format stay byte-for-byte identical forever. Fan-out is non-blocking (P0-A2 / D8): producer side does a select-with-default channel send; when the bounded channel (default 10000 records — `ATLAS_AUDIT_SINK_BUFFER_SIZE` override) overflows, the record is written to a NEW `audit_sink_failures` table (slice 036 four-policy append-only RLS pattern — `tenant_read FOR SELECT` + `tenant_write FOR INSERT WITH CHECK` under FORCE ROW LEVEL SECURITY, no UPDATE/DELETE policy, no UPDATE/DELETE grant for `atlas_app`) AND a slog ERROR is emitted (P0-A1: NEVER silent-drop). The writer goroutine batches writes (BatchFlushSize=100 records OR BatchFlushInterval=250ms quiet) with `O_APPEND|O_CREATE|O_WRONLY` for the standard concurrent-appender contract (logrotate `create` mode required — `copytruncate` breaks the open fd). New migration `20260518000000_audit_sink_failures.sql` (table + indexes + four-policy RLS + grants) is idempotent + reversible via `.down.sql`. Configuration: `ATLAS_AUDIT_SINK_PATH` (opt-in default — sink is no-op when unset, mirrors slice 121's `OTEL_EXPORTER_OTLP_ENDPOINT` pattern), `ATLAS_AUDIT_SINK_HMAC_KEY` (REQUIRED when path is set — D6 fail-fast: atlas refuses to boot if absent or shorter than 32 bytes, better to refuse than ship a silent integrity gap), `ATLAS_AUDIT_SINK_BUFFER_SIZE` (defaults 10000). Counters via `Sink.StatsSnapshot() (emitted, dropped, writeErrors, failureRows int64)` for ops dashboards. Test surface: 10 unit tests at `internal/audit/sink/sink_test.go` + 2 integration tests at `internal/audit/sink/integration_test.go` covering AC-7 (100 records all reach the sink within 5s with valid HMAC) and AC-8 (backpressure overflow lands in `audit_sink_failures` table — every drop produces exactly one fallback row, no panic, no silent drop). New operator docs at `docs/observability.md` document configuration + UID-separation pattern for the "external" property + logrotate gotchas + a reference Python HMAC verifier. JUDGMENT-type slice — decisions log at `docs/audit-log/126-external-audit-log-sink-decisions.md` records 10 design calls including D1 the sink-mechanism choice (picked option (a) JSONL-to-disk over the maintainer's filed lean of option (c) OTel logs via Collector; rationale: the OTel logs SDK is pre-1.0 alpha at `v0.19.0`, would add two unstable dependencies for a property that HMAC achieves transport-agnostically, JSONL composes with any operator log shipper, and the "external" property is achieved via filesystem UID ownership). Anti-criteria honored (P0): NEVER silent-drops on backpressure (A1); does NOT block the in-app INSERT on sink emit (A2); requires no new credential the deployment doesn't already have (A3); neutral `test-*` HMAC fixture only (A4); decisions log D1 documents all four mechanism options including the rejected ones (A5).
- **api:** `GET /v1/admin/audit-log/unified` — read-only UNION ALL across all nine per-domain audit-log tables ([#124](https://github.com/mgoodric/security-atlas/issues/124)) — coexists with the slice-062 `/v1/admin/audit-log` endpoint (different wire shape, different consumer set; slice 062 reads via `admin_audit_log_v` over 8 tables, slice 124 adds `aggregation_rule_audit_log` + `walkthrough_audit_log` directly via a new `internal/audit/unifiedlog/` aggregator package). Canonical Entry shape `{occurred_at, actor_id, tenant_id, kind, target_type, target_id, action, row_id, payload_json}`; `kind` enumerates `decision | evidence | exception | sample | audit_period | aggregation_rule | feature_flag | me | walkthrough`. Tenant isolation handed off to PostgreSQL RLS via `tenancy.ApplyTenant` under `atlas_app` (slice-124 P0-A4: no BYPASSRLS); the aggregator does NOT accept a tenant_id parameter (P0-A5: implicit from session context). Defense-in-depth role gate accepts admin OR auditor OR grc_engineer (D5 in `docs/audit-log/124-unified-audit-log-aggregation-decisions.md` — the v1 GRC operator persona needs visibility into their own program). 90-day request-window cap; 1000-row cursor-paginated page; cursor opaque base64 over `{occurred_at, kind, row_id}` (the audit-row's own UUID PK guarantees per-row uniqueness across the UNION — D6 in the decisions log). Migration `20260517000000_unified_audit_log.sql` adds the only missing composite index (`(tenant_id, created_at DESC)` on `aggregation_rule_audit_log`) and extends `me_audit_log.action` CHECK to include `'audit_log_query_unified'`. Every successful query writes one `me_audit_log` row with that action value (AC-10 meta-audit). New OPA matrix tests at `internal/authz/slice124_test.go` (admin allow / auditor allow / grc_engineer allow / viewer deny / control_owner deny / no-roles deny / auditor-write deny). Integration tests at `internal/api/adminauditlog/unified_integration_test.go` cover tenant-isolation across all 9 kinds (AC-9), meta-audit row written per query (AC-10), 1500-row cursor pagination walk (AC-11), 90-day window guard (AC-5), missing-param 400 (AC-5), no-eligible-role 403. EXPLAIN ANALYZE on the UNION shows index-scan paths on 8/9 base tables (`me_audit_log` planner-chosen Seq Scan at small row counts) — documented in the decisions log (D9). AFK-type slice. Unblocks slice 125 (frontend `/audit-log` page) and slice 126 (external audit-log sink). Anti-criteria honored (P0): read-only aggregator (P0-A1, P0-A8 type-system enforced — zero exported writer); no new physical audit-log table (P0-A2 — UNION is virtual); no schema change to the nine existing audit-log tables beyond the additive me_audit_log CHECK extension (P0-A3); no BYPASSRLS (P0-A4); no tenant_id parameter at API or aggregator surface (P0-A5); OPA role check on every code path (P0-A6); neutral `test-*` fixture tokens (P0-A7).

### Changed

- **api:** Admin `/api/admin/me` includes caller role list; `/audit-log` admits auditor + grc_engineer ([#130](https://github.com/mgoodric/security-atlas/issues/130)) — closes the slice-125 D9 follow-up. Backend slice-108 `GET /v1/me` profile shape gains a new additive `roles: []string` field sourced from `user_roles` via the shared `authz.DBRolesResolver` (same path the slice-035 OPA middleware uses — no duplicated SQL, P0-A1). The slice-060 BFF at `web/app/api/admin/me/route.ts` now proxies through `/v1/me` (instead of the admin-gated `/v1/admin/credentials` status probe — D2 in the decisions log) and forwards `{ is_admin, roles }` additively. Existing slice-060 consumers that read only `is_admin` continue to work unchanged (P0-A2 additive). Frontend slice-125 layout at `web/app/audit-log/layout.tsx` widens its server-side route guard from `is_admin === true` to `is_admin || roles.includes("auditor") || roles.includes("grc_engineer")` — the admit set matches slice-124's `HasUnifiedAuditLogRole` SQL exactly. Fail-closed posture (P0-A3): a missing or non-array `roles` field collapses to `[]` and admits only on `is_admin`. The slice-060 `/admin/*` layout is NOT widened (D5). Backend integration test surface (4 new tests at `internal/api/me/profile_integration_test.go`). 11-test vitest matrix on the BFF + 14-test predicate matrix on the layout. AFK-type slice; decisions log records 8 design calls.
- **backend:** Admin `/v1/admin/audit-log/unified` returns `actor_name` for callers with a users row ([#129](https://github.com/mgoodric/security-atlas/issues/129)) — closes the slice 125 D1 UX gap. The aggregator query in `internal/db/queries/unified_audit_log.sql` now LEFT JOINs onto `users` (keyed on `actor_id::uuid = users.id`, with a regex pre-filter that lets non-UUID actor_ids — credential ids like `key_foo`, system literals like `seeder` — fall through cleanly without raising) and projects `users.display_name` as the additive `actor_name` field. Wire shape is JSON `null` when no users row matches (purely additive — older clients that ignore unknown fields continue to work; P0-A3). Tenant isolation: two-leg guard — RLS on `users` (load-bearing, `atlas_app` role) + explicit `ON u.tenant_id = unified.tenant_id` predicate in the JOIN clause (defense-in-depth so an accidental BYPASSRLS upgrade cannot leak cross-tenant display names through this read). Frontend (`web/lib/api/audit-log.ts` + `web/app/audit-log/page-client.tsx`): the actor column now renders the resolved display name with the full `actor_id` exposed via tooltip; falls back to the slice-125 truncated-UUID render when `actor_name` is null OR undefined (P0-A6 graceful-degrade against older backends). New renderer helper `renderActorLabel` + `truncateActorId` extracted into `web/lib/api/audit-log.ts` so the contract can be vitest-locked without a JSX render harness. Tests: 3 new Go integration tests at `internal/api/adminauditlog/unified_integration_test.go` (AC-3 happy resolve, AC-4 null-on-wire + literal `"actor_name":null` body check, P0-A1 cross-tenant RLS isolation via a `leaked-secret` user seeded in Tenant B then audit-rowed in Tenant A); 9 new vitest cases at `web/lib/api/audit-log.test.ts`. sqlc regen on this slice: re-applied the three slice 109 hand-narrows. JUDGMENT-type slice; decisions log records 7 design calls.
- **frontend:** BFF forwards `atlas_session` cookie on `/api/me/sessions*` ([#110](https://github.com/mgoodric/security-atlas/issues/110)) — closes the slice 108 D4 UX gap. The three `/api/me/sessions*` BFF routes (`GET /api/me/sessions`, `DELETE /api/me/sessions`, `DELETE /api/me/sessions/{id}`) now forward the slice-034 `atlas_session` cookie alongside the existing `sa_session_token` bearer, so the slice-108 backend can flag the caller's current session row (`is_current: true`) and so the slice-103 `/settings` Active Sessions section can render a "current" badge. New `OIDC_SESSION_COOKIE = "atlas_session"` constant exported from `web/lib/auth.ts` with a doc comment pinning the narrow scope. New shared helper `web/app/api/me/sessions/_headers.ts::buildSessionsForwardHeaders(bearer, oidc)` colocated INSIDE the sessions directory on purpose (P0-A2: cookie scope is narrow — other BFF routes MUST NOT import it). Helper validates the cookie value against the slice-034 URL-safe-base64 alphabet (`/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]+$/`) and silently drops anything that fails, preventing a header-injection vector via a malformed cookie value (graceful degrade: platform sees no cookie → returns unflagged rows honestly). NO change to bearer-auth semantic — `sa_session_token` remains the primary auth carrier (P0-A2). NO change to `/api/me` profile or `/api/me/preferences` routes (P0-A1). NO backend change — slice 108 already reads `r.Cookie("atlas_session")`. New vitest coverage: 7 tests at `web/app/api/me/sessions/route.test.ts` (GET 401-no-bearer + GET forwards-both + GET omits-Cookie-when-absent + GET drops-malformed-injection + DELETE 401-no-bearer + DELETE forwards-both + DELETE omits-Cookie-when-absent); 3 tests at `web/app/api/me/sessions/[id]/route.test.ts` (DELETE 401-no-bearer + DELETE forwards-both-and-path + DELETE omits-Cookie-when-absent). Neutral `test-*` fixture tokens only (P0-A4 GitGuardian guard). AFK-type slice.

### Added

- **infra:** atlas OTel SDK integration (traces + metrics + Go runtime telemetry across HTTP/DB/NATS surfaces) ([#121](https://github.com/mgoodric/security-atlas/issues/121)) — wires the atlas-side OpenTelemetry SDK end-to-end so the receive-side backplane shipped by PR #234 (OTel Collector + Tempo + Prometheus at `deploy/observability/`) finally has a producer. New `internal/observability/otel/` package: `Init(ctx, opts)` configures `TracerProvider` + `MeterProvider` + the W3C TraceContext + Baggage propagators with OTel-standard env-var configuration (`OTEL_EXPORTER_OTLP_ENDPOINT`, `OTEL_EXPORTER_OTLP_PROTOCOL`, `OTEL_SERVICE_NAME`, `OTEL_RESOURCE_ATTRIBUTES`, `OTEL_TRACES_SAMPLER`, `OTEL_TRACES_SAMPLER_ARG`); no custom `ATLAS_OTEL_*` namespace (P0-A2). NO-OP when `OTEL_EXPORTER_OTLP_ENDPOINT` is unset (AC-2 / P0-A12): atlas serves traffic with zero telemetry overhead, the property that keeps the single-binary self-host story honest. HTTP server: `otelhttp.NewHandler(root, "atlas-http")` at the outermost middleware layer so every request including 401s gets a span; default attribute shape (`http.method`, `http.route`, `http.status_code`, `http.target`, `net.peer.ip`) — NO Authorization / Cookie / body recording (P0-A7 / P0-A8). High-frequency probes (`/health`, `/metrics`, `/v1/version`, `/v1/install-state`) excluded from span generation via `otelhttp.WithFilter` (AC-7). DB pool: all eight `pgxpool.New` call sites in `cmd/atlas/main.go` routed through `atlasotel.NewTracedPool`, which attaches the `otelpgx` tracer; `db.statement` keeps `$N` placeholders, parameter literal values are NEVER attribute-recorded (P0-A9). NATS: producer-side span on each `JetStreamPublisher.Publish` with W3C `traceparent` header injection; consumer extracts the header and starts a linked child span, so evidence-ingest is one trace from HTTP push → publisher span → consumer span → DB write. Message body NEVER on a span attribute — only subject + idempotency key (P0-A8 + AC-13). Go runtime metrics auto-emitted at 15s cadence via `go.opentelemetry.io/contrib/instrumentation/runtime` (`runtime.go.goroutines`, `runtime.go.gc.pause_total_ns`, `runtime.go.mem.heap_alloc`, `runtime.go.mem.heap_inuse`, `runtime.go.mem.lookups`, `runtime.go.cgo.calls`, `runtime.uptime`). Opt-in Prometheus `/metrics` fallback via `ATLAS_METRICS_FALLBACK_ENABLE=true` (DEFAULT OFF — P0-A3); when on, the route is bearer- and authz-exempt (same pattern as `/v1/version` from slice 092 — exact-path match) and operators MUST gate it at the network layer. Sampling defaults to `parentbased_traceidratio` at 10% (P0-A5); operators can crank for debugging via env-vars. Bounded async batch span processor only (P0-A4) — a slow OTel Collector can never block the request path. `cmd/atlas/main.go` startup line `atlas: opentelemetry: enabled endpoint=... sampler=... ...` (or `disabled` when off) so operators grepping logs can confirm state in one query (AC-4). New Go unit tests at `internal/observability/otel/otel_test.go` (5 tests: no-op-when-unset + service-name resolution × 2 + parseFloat + propagator composition); new integration tests at `internal/observability/otel/integration_test.go` (5 tests using `tracetest.InMemoryExporter`: bearer-token absent from any span attribute via the `test-bearer-sentinel-do-not-leak-12345` guard, NATS publisher → consumer trace-context linkage with parent-span-ID equality, NATS body absent from span attributes via `secret-payload-test-do-not-leak-abcdef` guard, publish-error stamps span Error status, consumer span uses SpanKindConsumer). New unit tests at `internal/api/metrics_endpoint_test.go` (4 tests: 404 when fallback disabled, 200 + Prometheus exposition when enabled, exemption scope is exact-path not prefix, span filter excludes the four high-frequency probes but keeps app paths). Docs: `deploy/docker/.env.example` adds the `OTEL_EXPORTER_OTLP_ENDPOINT` + `ATLAS_DEPLOYMENT_ENVIRONMENT` + `ATLAS_METRICS_FALLBACK_ENABLE` blocks with the network-layer-gating warning; `deploy/docker/docker-compose.yml` forwards every relevant OTEL\_\* env-var through the atlas service environment (no-op when unset); `deploy/observability/README.md` flips the Metrics / Traces status from "backplane ready" to "Working", documents the post-deploy smoke procedure (startup-log grep + curl traffic + Tempo / Prometheus / Grafana trace-to-logs verification). JUDGMENT-type slice — decisions log at `docs/audit-log/121-atlas-otel-sdk-decisions.md` records the 7 design calls (OTel SDK v1.34.0 + otelpgx v0.10.0 baseline; service.name=security-atlas resource attribute vs "atlas-http" otelhttp operation name; gRPC default OTLP protocol; hand-rolled NATS propagation over `nats.Header` since OTel ships no first-party NATS contrib; outermost otelhttp wrap; opt-in /metrics; deterministic sampler default). All AC pass surfaces green (`go test ./...` + `go test -tags=integration ./internal/observability/...`).
- **api:** `/v1/me/*` profile + preferences + sessions endpoints ([#108](https://github.com/mgoodric/security-atlas/issues/108)) — closes the slice 103 localStorage-fallback gap by shipping three endpoint families on the existing `/v1/me/*` namespace: `GET/PATCH /v1/me` (profile read + display_name/time_zone mutate, derived from `users` + credential), `GET/PATCH /v1/me/preferences` (per-event-per-channel notification matrix over a new `user_notification_preferences` table with `(tenant, user, event, channel)` UNIQUE + four-policy RLS), `GET /v1/me/sessions` + `DELETE /v1/me/sessions/{id}` + `DELETE /v1/me/sessions` (caller's slice-034 sessions, own-session-only with cookie-bridged `is_current` flag). New migration `20260516000003_me_endpoints.sql` adds the additive `users.time_zone TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT ''` column, the `user_notification_preferences` table, the append-only `me_audit_log` table (mirrors the slice 062 SELECT+INSERT-only invariant), and extends `admin_audit_log_v` via CREATE OR REPLACE to include the eighth branch. New `internal/auth/userprefs/` package wraps the preferences table with a whitelist-validated Upsert primitive (400 on unknown event/channel keys per ISC-A4; CHECK constraint at the DB layer is defense in depth). `internal/auth/sessions/` extended with `ListForUser`/`RevokeForUser`/`RevokeOthersForUser`; `internal/auth/users/` extended with `UpdateProfile`. `internal/api/me/` package gains `profile.go` + `preferences.go` + `sessions.go` over the existing `notifications.go`/`audit_period.go`. Apikeystore now threads `api_keys.issued_by` through to `cred.UserID` when present, so OIDC-provisioned bearers resolve to a real `users` row; bootstrap credentials with no users row degrade to a synthetic "API key" profile per P0-A1 (no fabrication). The handler reads the `atlas_session` cookie if present to flag `is_current`; bearer-only paths leave every row unflagged (honest — spillover slice 110 files BFF cookie-forwarding to close the UX gap). Frontend: `web/lib/api.ts` adds `getMe`/`patchMe`/`getMyPreferences`/`patchMyPreferences`/`listMySessions`/`revokeMySession` typed helpers; four new BFF routes under `web/app/api/me/{route.ts, preferences/route.ts, sessions/route.ts, sessions/[id]/route.ts}` follow the slice 060 cookie→bearer pattern; the slice-103 `/settings` page Profile + Notifications + Sessions sections cut over from localStorage fallbacks to the real endpoints (banners removed; the page now reflects server-side state cross-device). New Go integration tests at `internal/api/me/profile_integration_test.go` cover the happy path + bad-time_zone-400 + empty-diff-skips-audit + preferences default-all-enabled + 400-on-unknown-event + merge-semantic + own-sessions-only + cross-user-404 + RLS cross-tenant isolation on both preferences and sessions. New Go unit tests at `internal/auth/userprefs/userprefs_test.go` cover the whitelist + default-matrix. New vitest BFF coverage at `web/app/api/me/route.test.ts` (5 tests: missing-cookie 401, GET pass-through, PATCH body forwarding + 400 propagation). JUDGMENT-type slice — decisions log at `docs/audit-log/108-me-endpoints-decisions.md` records the 9 design calls (reuse users vs new user_profiles table, time_zone empty default, per-cell rows vs JSONB, cookie-bridged is_current, new me_audit_log + view extension, own-session-only scope, 400-on-unknown-keys, empty-diff skips audit, IssuedBy-to-UserID bridge). Spillover slice 110 filed for BFF atlas_session cookie forwarding. sqlc regen on this slice: re-applied the slice 109 hand-narrows (no new hand-narrows introduced).
- **frontend:** `/settings` user-facing page ([#103](https://github.com/mgoodric/security-atlas/issues/103)) — closes the last remaining audit-finding F-4 case (the `/settings` link in the sidebar previously 404'd). New page at `web/app/(authed)/settings/page.tsx` renders the five user-facing sections per the design captured in slice 093 (`Plans/mockups/settings.html` + `Plans/canvas/12-ui-fill-in-design-decisions.md` §4): Profile · Appearance · Notifications · Personal API tokens · Active sessions. P0-A1 honored: tenant-wide settings stay at `/admin/*`; the cross-link `Tenant administration -> /admin` is visible only to admin role via the slice 097 D3 `getSessionMe().is_admin` pattern. P0-A2 honored: the personal-token plaintext-once invariant is encoded in a dedicated reducer at `web/app/(authed)/settings/token-state.ts` — the bearer is held in `useReducer` state for the duration of the `issued` callout and discarded on DISMISS or a second ISSUED (rotation case); `JSON.stringify`-based vitest assertions lock down that no leftover plaintext survives in state. P0-A3 honored: admin RBAC is enforced at the backend (`GET /v1/admin/credentials` returns 403 for non-admins per slice 034); non-admins see an affordance card pointing at `/admin/api-keys` rather than a token list. Notification toggles persist to localStorage with a banner pointing at the spillover (`localStorage` key shape `security-atlas.settings.notif.{event}.{channel}`). The Profile / Notifications / Sessions sections render banners explaining the corresponding backend endpoint is pending — **spillover slice 108** files `GET /v1/me` profile, `GET/PATCH /v1/me/preferences`, and `GET /v1/me/sessions` + `DELETE /v1/me/sessions/{id}` as one cohesive `/v1/me/*` surface (mirrors slice 098 -> 104 + slice 101 -> 107 spillover precedent). Reuses the existing slice-060 `/api/admin/credentials` BFF for the token flow — no new BFF routes added, no shared `web/lib/api.ts` modifications. New vitest unit coverage at `web/app/(authed)/settings/theme.test.ts` (10 tests: parseTheme normalization, round-trip persistence, pinned-key contract, reload survival) and `web/app/(authed)/settings/token-state.test.ts` (8 tests: initial state, ISSUED action, DISMISS clears plaintext via JSON.stringify check, rotation overwrites bearer, exhaustiveness). Playwright spec at `web/e2e/settings.spec.ts` follows the slice-079 quarantine pattern pending the slice-082 seed-data harness; AC bodies preserved verbatim as a reviewable contract including a P0-A2 reload-no-plaintext assertion. AFK-type slice. Anti-criteria honored (P0): does NOT show tenant-wide settings; does NOT re-display token plaintext after dismissal; does NOT grant non-admins access to admin-scoped endpoints; does NOT migrate any `/admin/*` page to `/settings`; does NOT use vendor-prefixed tokens in test fixtures.
- **api:** `GET /v1/policies?include=ack_rate` join + `/policies` real ack-rate cells ([#107](https://github.com/mgoodric/security-atlas/issues/107)) — additive `?include=ack_rate` query parameter on `GET /v1/policies` returns the policy list LEFT JOINed to the per-policy acknowledgment-rate cell. Implemented as a **single SQL query** with two correlated scalar subqueries (denominator + numerator) — explicitly NOT a per-policy loop calling `AckStore.Rate` (the slice exists to avoid the 50-call fan-out anti-pattern slice 101 P0-A2 forbids). Predicates mirror `CountRequiredRoleUsersForVersion` (denominator) + `CountFreshAcksForVersion` (numerator) verbatim — same role-intersect EXISTS clause, same freshness cutoff parameter, same `api_keys` slice-034 stand-in. Wire shape: `{policies: [{...policyWire, ack_rate: cell | null}], count}` — additive; callers that omit `?include=ack_rate` continue to receive the v1 policy-only shape unchanged. Non-published policies (draft / under_review / approved / superseded) return `ack_rate: null` because the SQL CASE WHEN status='published' branch did not fire — the wire layer renders the em-dash branch honestly. Published policies with zero denominator (no required-role users) return a populated cell with `percent: null` (matches the slice-023 `rateResponse` semantic — consumers distinguish "0% acknowledged" from "no required-role members exist"). Backend constitutional invariants honored: #6 (RLS — the join runs in a `tenancy.ApplyTenant` transaction, integration test asserts Tenant A's request never sees Tenant B's ack rows); #9 (manual evidence first-class — the join is uniform whether the policy was manually authored or imported from a vendor template). Frontend: `web/lib/api.ts` hard-codes `?include=ack_rate` on both `listPolicies` (BFF) and `fetchPoliciesList` (browser) — mirrors slice 104's hard-coded `?include=state` for anchors; `web/app/(authed)/policies/page.tsx` removes the "awaiting publish or ack-rate join" placeholder copy and renders real `<Progress>` cells when the joined cell is populated. New Go unit tests at `internal/api/policies/ack_rate_unit_test.go` (6 cases: published with acks, non-published null-branch across 4 statuses, zero-denominator percent-null, parser across plain/CSV/repeated/whitespace, embedded `policyWire` shape preservation, orphan_policy warning); new Go integration tests at `internal/api/policies/ack_rate_integration_test.go` (5 cases: omitted-include shape unchanged, draft-only all-null branch, published-with-ack populated cell, RLS round-trip Tenant A vs Tenant B, cross-check that joined math equals per-policy `/v1/policies/{id}/acknowledgment-rate` output). New vitest case in `web/app/api/policies/route.test.ts` pinning the `?include=ack_rate` query-string forward. Spillover from slice 101.

The page reuses slice 098's shared list shell verbatim — `ListPage` + `FilterPills` + `ListTable` + `EmptyState` + `ListLoadingSkeleton` from `@/components/list` — proving the 098 extraction holds across the 098/099/100/101/102 consumer set. The mockup's `Linked control` and `Ack status` pills are deferred per slice 101 D2 — the wire shape has no framework field for `Linked control` (requires `linked_control_ids` → `controls` → `framework_versions` join), and `Ack status` requires `?include=ack_rate` which is not on main. Ack-rate column renders an em-dash placeholder honestly today (slice 098 D1 precedent) — **spillover slice 107** files `GET /v1/policies?include=ack_rate` to lift the per-row fan-out anti-pattern that the slice text + P0-A2 reject (N policies × per-row rate call). When slice 107 lands the page's em-dash branch flips to the wired-but-dormant `<Progress>` branch with no further frontend work. Color-coded progress thresholds per AC-9 + the slice 101 decisions log: green ≥95% / amber 70-94% / red <70% — derived from the SOC 2 CC1.4 norm where >90% acknowledgment is "compliant". Empty state bifurcates by reason: true zero-state (no policies at all) shows "No policies published yet" with `Scaffold five foundational policies` CTA per design doc §2; the CTA lands on `/admin/credentials` as a placeholder (slice 100 P0-A2 precedent — no in-list policy-create UI per P0-A4). Filter-induced empty shows "No policies match these filters" with `Clear filters`. Tenant isolation per Invariant 6 — BFF reads bearer cookie + forwards as Authorization header, never accepts tenant_id from the client; RLS denies cross-tenant reads at the DB layer. Vitest covers ack-rate formatter + color-band thresholds (25 tests at `web/app/(authed)/policies/ack-rate.test.ts`), row-narrowing + uniqueOwners normalization (14 tests at `web/app/(authed)/policies/filters.test.ts`) and the BFF route (3 tests at `web/app/api/policies/route.test.ts` — 401/bearer-forward/upstream-error). Playwright spec at `web/e2e/policies-list.spec.ts` follows the slice-079 quarantine pattern pending the slice-082 seed-data harness; AC bodies preserved verbatim as a reviewable contract including a P0-A2 fan-out negative-assertion case. Form binds DIRECTLY to the slice-019 `createReq` wire shape (`internal/api/risks/handlers.go`) — no invented fields per P0-A4. Required fields per AC-2: title, description (optional), category (7-value enum), methodology (default `nist_800_30`, 5-value enum), treatment (default `mitigate`, 4-value enum), treatment_owner, and a 5x5 inherent score widget (likelihood + impact dropdowns 1..5) that serializes into `inherent_score: {likelihood, impact}` — the shape `severityOf()` reads downstream so the new row's severity scalar lights up immediately on the list view. Optional fields not enumerated in AC-2 (residual_score, review_due_at, accepted_until, accepter, instrument_reference, linked_control_ids) are omitted from the UI rather than fabricated — a future slice adds the richer editor. 5x5 score widget uses two native `<select>` dropdowns (vendor-form precedent: shadcn Select primitive does not exist in `web/components/ui/` yet) with a live severity readout ("likelihood × impact"). On success the page invalidates the TanStack `["risks", "list"]` query (AC-4) so slice 100's list refetches with the new row, then router-pushes to `/risks`. On 4xx the upstream `{error}` string surfaces in an inline Alert above the submit row (AC-5) and the form state is preserved — the user fixes the flagged field and resubmits without re-typing. **BFF extension**: `web/app/api/risks/route.ts` adds a POST handler that forwards the bearer cookie + JSON body to `/v1/risks` upstream verbatim (slice 098/100 cookie→bearer pattern); GET handler unchanged. **CTA re-point**: slice 100's `/risks` empty-state CTA flips from `router.push("/admin")` to `router.push("/risks/new")`. **`web/lib/api.ts`** appends a `RiskCreateInput` type + `createRisk(bearer, body)` server-side fn for symmetry with `createVendor` (slice 024). Constitutional invariants honored: #6 (tenant isolation) — the BFF carries the bearer cookie; the platform enforces RLS on the insert path; the form never passes tenant_id. AI-assist boundary: no AI on this surface — pure CRUD form. Vitest covers POST success (201), 4xx propagation, and missing-cookie 401 at `web/app/api/risks/route.test.ts` (3 new tests; full file now 6 tests). Playwright spec at `web/e2e/risks-create.spec.ts` follows the slice-079 quarantine pattern pending the slice-082 seed-data harness; AC bodies preserved verbatim as a reviewable contract. - **frontend:** `/audits` period list view ([#102](https://github.com/mgoodric/security-atlas/issues/102)) — closes the second remaining audit-finding F-4 case (the plural `/audits` link in the sidebar previously 404'd). New page at `web/app/(authed)/audits/page.tsx` renders the tenant's `audit_periods` as a table per the design captured in slice 093 (`Plans/mockups/audits.html` + `Plans/canvas/12-ui-fill-in-design-decisions.md` §2/3/6/7/8). Row source is `periodWire` from `GET /v1/audit-periods` via a new tenant-scoped BFF at `web/app/api/audits/route.ts` (slice 094 cookie→bearer pattern). The mockup's "Sample size" column is intentionally OMITTED — `periodWire` does NOT carry it, and the slice rejects invented columns per P0-A4. Frozen periods render a lock icon next to the status pill with a `title=` tooltip showing `frozen at YYYY-MM-DD by <actor>` (AC-6). Non-frozen periods whose `period_end` is within 30 days of `now` render an amber pulsing dot + "Xd left" label as an early-fieldwork cue (AC-6). The page reuses slice 098's shared list shell verbatim — `ListPage` + `FilterPills` + `ListTable` + `EmptyState` + `ListLoadingSkeleton` from `@/components/list` — proving the 098 extraction holds across the 099/100/101/102 consumer set. Filter row is horizontal pills (framework + status + year, P0-A2 of 098 — no left sidebar); status pill enumerates the broader forward-looking status vocabulary (`open` · `in_progress` · `frozen` · `closed` · `planned`) even though the v1 DB CHECK constraint only allows `{'open','frozen'}`, so the page is forward-compatible when the platform lifts the constraint. Empty state bifurcates by reason: true zero-state (no periods at all) shows "No audit periods yet" with `Create audit period` CTA per design doc §2 audits row; filter-induced empty shows "No periods match these filters" with `Clear filters`. Frozen periods are read-only in the list (P0-A2); the `Create audit period` CTA is a placeholder routing to `/admin` (P0-A3 — no in-list create UI). Row click navigates to `/audits/[id]` placeholder for the per-period detail slice. No collision with the singular `/audit/[controlId]` route from slice 042 — different file, different segment, different goal (period index vs auditor walk-through per design doc §6 disambiguation). No collision with the existing `/api/audit/periods` BFF (which forwards `/v1/me/audit-periods` for caller-assignment workspace scoping); `/api/audits` forwards `/v1/audit-periods` for tenant-wide period index scoping. Tenant isolation per Invariant 6 — BFF reads bearer cookie + forwards as Authorization header, never accepts tenant_id from the client; RLS denies cross-tenant reads at the DB layer. Audit-period freezing per Invariant 10 — frozen periods locked in the list, lock visualization is the contractual marker. Vitest covers row-narrowing (16 tests at `web/app/(authed)/audits/filters.test.ts`), presentation helpers (36 tests at `web/app/(authed)/audits/format.test.ts` — status-pill color, dot-pulse logic, frozen-icon visibility, days-until-end formatting, urgent-cue threshold, tooltip generation, range labels, frozen-meta labels) and the BFF route (4 tests at `web/app/api/audits/route.test.ts` — 401/bearer-forward/upstream-error/RBAC-passthrough). Playwright spec at `web/e2e/audits-list.spec.ts` follows the slice-079 quarantine pattern pending the slice-082 seed-data harness; AC bodies preserved verbatim as a reviewable contract. - **api:** `GET /v1/anchors?include=state` join + `/controls` real state cells ([#104](https://github.com/mgoodric/security-atlas/issues/104)) — additive `?include=state` query parameter on `GET /v1/anchors` returns the anchor catalog LEFT JOINed to the tenant's worst-state-wins per-anchor rollup over the slice-012 `control_evaluations` ledger. Implemented as a **single CTE+join SQL query** — explicitly NOT a per-anchor loop calling `eval.Engine.ControlState` (the slice exists to avoid the 1,400-call fan-out anti-pattern that slice 098 surfaced). The CTE picks the latest evaluation per (tenant, control, scope_cell) via `DISTINCT ON` (mirrors slice 012's `ListLatestControlEvaluations` pattern), then aggregates worst-state-wins per anchor: `fail` > `inconclusive` > `pass` > `na` for the result column, `expired` > `stale` > `no_evidence` > `fresh` for freshness, `MAX` for `last_observed_at`. Anchors with no tenant control return `state: null` (the empty-state branch is honest — no fabrication, slice 098 P0-A1 carries through). Wire shape: `{anchors: [{...anchorWire, state: stateWire | null}]}` — additive; callers that omit `?include=state` continue to receive the v1 anchor-only shape unchanged. Both the latest-version list path and the `?framework_version_id=` path support the extension. Backend constitutional invariants honored: #6 (RLS — the join runs in a `tenancy.ApplyTenant` transaction, integration test asserts Tenant A's request never sees Tenant B's state); #2 (engine is sole writer of `control_evaluations` — this is a pure read over the engine's output table, not a parallel computation); #1 (rollup is per ANCHOR, not per framework). Frontend: `web/lib/api.ts` adds `AnchorWithState` + `listAnchorsWithState`; `web/app/api/controls/route.ts` (BFF) now calls the joined endpoint; `web/app/(authed)/controls/page.tsx` removes the `state: null` placeholder mapping and renders real state cells (the `—` placeholder remains for the legitimate null-state branch). New Go unit tests at `internal/api/anchors/state_unit_test.go` (5 cases: pass-through, no-control-null, no-observation, two-controls-pre-aggregated, include-parser); new Go integration tests at `internal/api/anchors/state_integration_test.go` (4 cases: omitted-include shape, empty-tenant null branch, worst-state-wins + RLS round-trip Tenant A vs Tenant B, design-doc column set); new vitest case in `web/app/api/controls/route.test.ts` pinning the joined-shape pass-through. JUDGMENT-type slice — decisions log at `docs/audit-log/104-anchors-include-state-decisions.md` records the 9 design calls. Spillover from slice 098.
- **frontend:** `/controls` list view + shared list shell ([#098](https://github.com/mgoodric/security-atlas/issues/098)) — closes audit finding F-4 (the `/controls` link in the sidebar previously 404'd). New page at `web/app/(authed)/controls/page.tsx` renders the SCF anchor library as a table per the design captured in slice 093 (`Plans/mockups/controls.html` + `Plans/canvas/12-ui-fill-in-design-decisions.md` §1/2/3/7/8). Row source is `anchorWire` from `GET /v1/anchors` via a new tenant-scoped BFF at `web/app/api/controls/route.ts` (slice 094 cookie→bearer pattern). The page consumes the **new reusable list shell** at `web/components/list/` — five generic primitives (`ListPage`, `FilterPills`, `ListTable`, `EmptyState`, `ListLoadingSkeleton`) intentionally scoped domain-agnostic so the next four list-view slices (099 evidence / 100 risks / 101 policies / 102 audits) consume them from a single barrel import. Filter row is horizontal pills (P0-A2: no left sidebar); loading is 3 shimmer rows (P0-A3: no spinner); empty-state copy "No controls match these filters" with `Clear filters` CTA (filter-induced — every tenant has SCF anchors). State columns (`result` / `freshness_status` / `last_observed_at`) render `—` honestly until backend extension lands — slice 098 D1 records the gap, **spillover slice 104** files `GET /v1/anchors?include=state` to lift the per-row fan-out anti-pattern that the slice text itself rejected (1,400 anchors × per-row state call). Vitest covers the row-narrowing + filter-state computation (16 tests at `web/app/(authed)/controls/filters.test.ts`) and the BFF route (3 tests at `web/app/api/controls/route.test.ts`). Playwright spec at `web/e2e/controls-list.spec.ts` follows the slice-079 quarantine pattern pending the slice-082 seed-data harness. AFK-type slice.
- **frontend:** Compliance calendar with ICS export ([#094](https://github.com/mgoodric/security-atlas/issues/094)) — cross-business view of upcoming compliance dates at `/calendar`. Aggregates four event types only (audit periods, exception expirations, policy reviews, periodic control reviews) per P0-A1; per-evidence-record freshness drift and framework-version supersession are deliberately out of scope. Backend: new `internal/api/calendar/` with three handlers (`GET /v1/calendar` JSON list, `GET /v1/calendar.ics` RFC 5545 feed, `POST /v1/calendar/subscription` token mint). ONE UNION ALL query in `internal/db/queries/calendar.sql` over `audit_periods` + `exceptions` + `policies` + `controls` (with a LEFT JOIN LATERAL onto `control_evaluations` for `last_evaluated_at`). Cadence math derives `next_due_at = last_evaluated_at + cadence_interval` where the cadence interval is the same `FreshnessMaxAge` mapping the slice-012 evaluation engine uses (one source of truth — decision D1). Default to rolling-window (D2). Never-evaluated control with a defined cadence emits an event at `now()` with `status=overdue` per AC-2c. ICS auth is a per-user opaque URL token (D3): `POST /v1/calendar/subscription` mints a random 32-byte token via `credstore.Issue`, stores `sha256(token)` in `api_keys`, scopes via `AllowedKinds=[calendar.read.v1]` so a leaked calendar URL token cannot be used as a general bearer; the calendar.ics handler is exempt from the upstream bearer-header middleware and authenticates the URL token inline. New migration `20260516000002_policies_next_review_at.sql` adds `policies.next_review_at TIMESTAMPTZ NULL` (D4) — no change to existing write paths; operators populate the value directly or via a future admin UI. RLS enforced at the DB layer (slice 033 pattern); integration test ISC-94-1/94-2 asserts cross-tenant isolation on both the exception and control-cadence paths. Truncation flag fires at the 500-event ceiling with a `next_from` cursor (D7). Frontend: new `/calendar` route under `web/app/(authed)/calendar/page.tsx` with hand-rolled agenda + month-grid views (no calendar-library dependency per P0-A6 / D6), URL-driven type filter, overdue-cue red dot for control events, "Subscribe in calendar" button copies the ICS URL to clipboard. New top-nav entry between Dashboard and Metrics (D8). BFF proxies under `web/app/api/calendar/{route.ts, subscription/route.ts}` follow the slice-040 pattern. Vitest covers both BFF routes (5 tests). Go integration tests (7 tests) cover RLS isolation, cadence math both branches, truncation, ICS validity, and ICS auth rejection cases. Playwright spec at `web/e2e/calendar.spec.ts` follows the slice-079 quarantine pattern pending the slice-082 seed-data harness. AFK-type slice — decisions log at `docs/audit-log/094-compliance-calendar-decisions.md` records the 8 design calls.
- **frontend:** Metrics dashboard + cascade-tree view ([#097](https://github.com/mgoodric/security-atlas/issues/097)) — the frontend that consumes the slice-076 metrics-catalog backbone. New route `/dashboards/metrics` shows board-level metric cards (latest value, target, 90-day SVG sparkline, color-coded threshold badge); each card expands a vertical indent-and-rule cascade tree (decision D1) walking `GET /v1/metrics/cascade?level=board&depth=3` and reassembling the flat node list into a tree client-side. New route `/dashboards/metrics/[id]` shows the metric definition, immediate parents/children, the observation series as an inline-SVG line chart with target/warning/critical horizontal overlays, the target configuration panel, and a manual-input audit-trail. Admin-only `Submit value` modal (decision D3) gated via `getSessionMe().is_admin` (reuses the slice-043 pattern) that POSTs to `/v1/metrics/{id}/inputs` and invalidates the per-metric observation query so the new row appears immediately. Backend additive only — no slice-076 API changes (P0-A1). No new chart-library dependency (decision D2): inline-SVG sparkline + line-chart components colocated under `web/components/dashboards-metrics/` using shadcn theme tokens. New BFF routes under `web/app/api/metrics/**` follow the slice-040 dashboardProxy pattern. New shadcn Dialog primitive at `web/components/ui/dialog.tsx` wrapping `@base-ui/react/dialog`. Vitest suite at `web/lib/api/metrics.test.ts` covers the pure cascade-tree reassembly + threshold-badge color logic (22 tests, all green). Playwright spec at `web/e2e/metrics-dashboard.spec.ts` follows the slice-079 quarantine pattern pending the slice-082 seed-data harness. JUDGMENT-type slice — decisions log at `docs/audit-log/097-metrics-dashboard-cascade-view-decisions.md` records the three subjective calls.
- **catalog:** Metrics catalog + cascade + observation store ([#076](https://github.com/mgoodric/security-atlas/issues/076)) — 5-table data model (`metrics_catalog`, `metric_cascade_edges`, `metric_observations`, `metric_targets`, `metric_inputs`) seeded from 8 YAML files at `catalogs/metrics/` (40 curated metrics across board / program / team cascades). 8 starter Go evaluators in `internal/metrics/eval/` run on a 15-minute cron (`ATLAS_METRICS_INTERVAL` env override). Read/write API: `GET /v1/metrics`, `GET /v1/metrics/cascade`, `GET /v1/metrics/{id}`, `GET /v1/metrics/{id}/observations`, `POST /v1/metrics/{id}/inputs`, `GET /v1/metrics/{id}/target`, `PUT /v1/metrics/{id}/target`. Insert trigger on `metric_inputs` replicates each manual entry to `metric_observations` so reads serve a unified series. Singleton-tenant-agnostic RLS pattern (slice 068) for the catalog + edges; four-policy RLS for tenant-scoped tables; append-only RLS for observations + inputs. Mkdocs site gains a "Measuring your program" page + an auto-generated reference (`just metrics-reference`). Follow-on dashboard slice filed at #097 (slice doc's 078 reference was stale). JUDGMENT-type slice — decisions log at `docs/audit-log/076-metrics-catalog-cascade-decisions.md` records the 16 design calls.
- **infra:** Security HTTP headers middleware ([#087](https://github.com/mgoodric/security-atlas/issues/087)) — new `internal/api/securityheaders/` package applied as the FIRST `root.Use(...)` in the chi chain. Sets HSTS, X-Content-Type-Options, X-Frame-Options, Referrer-Policy on every response (including 401s + auth-exempt `/health` + `/auth/*` + `/login`). CSP ships as `Content-Security-Policy-Report-Only` initially (Next.js inline-script hydration would violate `script-src 'self'` enforcement); the trajectory toward enforced mode is documented in `docs/audit-log/087-security-http-headers-middleware-decisions.md`. Remediates the MEDIUM-HIGH finding from the 2026-Q2 security audit.
- **infra:** Dependency vulnerability scanning ([#089](https://github.com/mgoodric/security-atlas/issues/089)) — three new informational CI jobs in `.github/workflows/ci.yml`: `Go · govulncheck` (Go call-graph-aware CVE detection, pinned `govulncheck@v1.1.3`), `Frontend · npm audit` (runtime-shipped JS deps in `web/`, `--omit=dev --audit-level=high`), and `Container · Trivy scan` (OS-package CVEs in the locally-built atlas image, `aquasecurity/trivy-action@0.28.0` with `ignore-unfixed: true`). All three follow the slice-061/069 stub-job pattern and are NOT added to `.github/branch-protection.json`'s required-checks list — promotion-to-required is a future slice once cadence + false-positive rate are observed. HIGH+CRITICAL is the unified fail threshold. Reports upload as workflow artifacts for maintainer triage. Per-scanner suppression mechanism + threshold rationale documented in `docs/audit-log/089-dependency-vulnerability-scanning-decisions.md`. Remediates the MEDIUM finding from the 2026-Q2 security audit (the last open finding from that audit).
- **frontend:** Mockups for six missing top-level pages ([#093](https://github.com/mgoodric/security-atlas/issues/093)) — six static HTML wireframes added under `Plans/mockups/` for the previously-404'ing top-level routes: `controls.html`, `evidence.html`, `risks.html`, `policies.html`, `audits.html`, `settings.html`. Each mockup is a self-contained Tailwind-via-CDN file matching the existing dashboard/board-pack chrome (sticky top-bar + 56px left sidebar + main panel with horizontal filter row + table + empty state + loading skeleton). All column sets derived from verified backend wire shapes (`anchorWire` + `stateWire`, `recordWire` + `receiptWire`, `riskWire`, `policyWire` + `rateResponse`, `periodWire`, `me/*` + `admincreds.ListItem`) per P0-A5; no invented endpoints. The dashboard sidebar is updated to the new canonical nav order (Dashboard · Controls · Evidence · Risks · Audits · Policies · Vendors · Board Packs · Settings · Admin) so the user sees consistent placement when navigating by mockup-flipping; the four sidebar-less mockups (control / board-pack / questionnaire) are unchanged per P0-A3. `Plans/mockups/index.html` gains a "v1 fill-in pages" section with one tile per mockup, each marked "design only — implementation pending". Design decisions captured in `Plans/canvas/12-ui-fill-in-design-decisions.md` (top-nav order rationale, empty-state + loading-skeleton patterns, /settings user-only vs /admin tenant scope, /risks list vs /risks/hierarchy co-existence, /audits plural vs /audit/[controlId] singular). Unblocks six follow-up per-page implementation slices to be drafted post-merge.

### Changed

- **infra:** sqlc toolchain pinned to v1.31.1 + regen reset ([#109](https://github.com/mgoodric/security-atlas/issues/109)) — closes the slice 106 P1 finding ("sqlc toolchain drift produces silent regen regressions"). `SQLC_VERSION := "v1.31.1"` pinned in `justfile`; new `sqlc-version-check` recipe gated as a dependency of `sqlc-generate`; CLAUDE.md tech-stack table + CONTRIBUTING.md Prerequisites + Local CI parity subsection all reference the pin. Root cause documented in `docs/audit-log/109-sqlc-toolchain-pin-decisions.md`: slice 065 wrapped `CREATE TYPE` in idempotent `DO $$ BEGIN ... END $$;` blocks for self-host bundle re-apply safety, but sqlc v1.31.1 cannot parse `CREATE TYPE` inside a DO block — every enum-typed SELECT column degrades to `interface{}` on regen, dropping typed enum constants downstream callers depend on. Fix: new tool-input-only file `internal/db/sqlc-schema/_enums.sql` declares every enum as a bare `CREATE TYPE` and is listed FIRST in `sqlc.yaml`'s `schema:` array (NEVER applied to a live DB; the justfile migration runner globs `migrations/sql/*.sql` only). The DO-block re-declarations that follow are silently tolerated as duplicates by sqlc. Slice 076's `internal/db/dbx/models_metrics.go` hand-split is RETIRED — the regen places metric types back into the canonical `models.go`. Two narrow hand-narrows preserved (`AckDenominator`/`AckNumerator` in `policies.sql.go` × slice 107; `StateResult`/`StateFreshnessStatus` × 2 row types in `scf_anchors.sql.go` × slice 104) — each annotated in place with a pointer to the decisions log. Slice 106's hand-extension of `ListEvidencePaged` is retired; the natural sqlc-emitted form is now canonical. New informational CI job `Go · sqlc generate diff` added with `continue-on-error: true` (NOT in required-checks per P0-A3). No behavioral change — `go test ./...` passes with zero test edits.

- **infra:** Pre-push hook now also runs `npm run lint -w web` ([#083](https://github.com/mgoodric/security-atlas/issues/083)) — follow-on to slice 081 D3. Adds a `local` `npm-lint-web` hook to `.pre-commit-config.yaml` (`stages: [pre-push]`, `files: ^web/`, `language: system`, `pass_filenames: false`) so frontend ESLint errors block locally before CI's `Frontend · lint` job (slice 078) catches them. Hook is skipped on backend-only pushes via the `^web/` files predicate. Emergency bypass via `git push --no-verify` is unchanged (P0-A1). Decisions log at `docs/audit-log/083-pre-push-hook-add-lint-decisions.md` records the AC-3 deliberate-failure test result (`react/jsx-key` error → push exit code 1, push not transmitted).
- **frontend:** Root route `/` now redirects instead of rendering the stock create-next-app template that shipped with the slice-005 scaffold ([#091](https://github.com/mgoodric/security-atlas/issues/091)). Server-component handler at `web/app/page.tsx` reads `SESSION_COOKIE` from `@/lib/auth` and emits a 307 to `/dashboard` for authenticated requests, `/login?from=/` for unauthenticated requests. Two destinations only — no third "/onboarding" branch (P0-A2). No client-side JS, no rendered UI, no Vercel/Next.js branding remains. New Playwright spec at `web/e2e/root-redirect.spec.ts` asserts both branches at the 307-response level plus a belt-and-suspenders navigation-follow case; uses neutral `test-*` fixture tokens only (P0-A7). Stock-template SVGs were already absent from `web/public/` at slice start (AC-6 no-op). Verified locally: `curl -I http://localhost:3015/` returns 307 → `/login?from=%2F`; with `Cookie: sa_session_token=…` returns 307 → `/dashboard`.

### Fixed

- **infra (slice 088):** atlas-cli outbound HTTP calls now carry an explicit per-call timeout via the new `cmd/atlas-cli/cmdhttp` package (10s for feature-flag reads, 30s for credential reset-bootstrap). Resolves the Q2 2026 security audit MEDIUM finding — the package-level-default HTTP client has no timeout and a hung atlas server would previously stall the CLI indefinitely. AC-4 grep gate enforces zero residual package-level-default-client references under `cmd/atlas-cli/`.
- **infra (slice 092):** version display end-to-end fix. Two bugs combined to make the deployed UI's footer report `v(none)` / `unknown`: (a) `.github/workflows/container-publish.yml`'s `docker/build-push-action@v7` step did not pass `build-args` for `VERSION`/`COMMIT`/`BUILD_TIME`, so the Dockerfile's `dev`/`none`/`unknown` defaults were baked into every published image; (b) `web/proxy.ts` (the Next.js 16 request interceptor, renamed from `middleware.ts`) had no exemption for `/api/version`, so every BFF call to the intentionally-public version endpoint returned 307 → `/login`. The workflow now passes all three build-args from `steps.meta.outputs.version` + `github.sha` + a computed RFC3339 UTC `BUILD_TIME`; `proxy.ts` exempts exactly `/api/version` (exact-equality match, P0-A1 forbids broadening). New vitest unit test at `web/proxy.test.ts` (10 cases enumerating the exempt path + adjacent-prefix attack surface + the AC-7 negative case `/api/admin/me` still 307s) plus new Playwright e2e at `web/e2e/version-public.spec.ts`. AC-3 reduces to "verify on first release after merge" — the publish-time smoke deferred to keep the diff minimal per the slice scope. Decisions log: `docs/audit-log/092-version-display-end-to-end-decisions.md`.
